## Explicit estimator seed contract — 2026-09-14

Moved from the preceding planning-only work to training-chain implementation. Binary baseline and joint runners accept optional `random_seed` (default 20), reject booleans/non-integers/out-of-range seeds before fitting and during cost preflight, and pass the actual value into estimator construction. Supported paths: binary logistic/shallow tree/frequency; joint multinomial/cost-sensitive/conditional-three/frequency. Model cards record the requested seed, its actual role, and explicitly deny independent market evidence. LBFGS does not use this random state; empirical frequency is deterministic; shallow trees use random feature permutation/split ties. Different seed identifiers therefore do not imply different learned models or independent market observations.

Binary saved-chain replay preserves the explicit seed; joint inference replay checks seed/card consistency without refitting. Existing comparison scopes still lock non-varied plan fields: this change does not silently authorize cross-seed campaigns, aggregate finalists, change frozen budgets, or accept H04. Configuration/fold/seed aggregation remains required.

Validation under the final code: joint run/model/conditional/frequency/family-campaign tests **26 passed in 40.53s**; baseline model/run/replay, multifold and development-frontier tests **40 passed in 62.10s**. Earlier binary/policy targeted run **36 passed in 27.23s** preceded joint edits. These counts overlap and are not a full-suite count. All fits were synthetic fixtures; no real-market efficacy claim, production changes, scheduler changes or formal stage promotion. Historical saved bundles whose computational source pins no longer match remain ineligible for current-code replay; they are not overwritten or silently re-certified.

Next: implement explicit registered cross-seed comparison and configuration-level aggregation with complete fold/seed membership, deterministic-repeat disclosures, budget accounting and missing/failed-run handling. Formal H01/H02/H03 evidence gaps remain separate and unresolved; the full harness objective is not complete.

This is an execution record, not evidence of improved stock selection.

## Announcement normalization and restructuring distinction — 2026-09-14 00:37

Added `distribution_normalize.py` and three regression tests. Only records identical in every acquired raw field except proposal/decision `ann_date` are collapsed. Report period, implementation announcement, all cash/bonus rates and payment/listing dates must match exactly. Null never equals zero; different report periods are not summed; conflicting variants remain separate. Conservative terms-date uses the latest retained announcement date and is not represented as proven vendor availability. Every original row has a lineage mapping and content hash.

Actual run normalized 14,926 source rows into 14,427 event rows, collapsing 499 announcement repetitions while preserving all 14,926 lineage entries. 38 conflicting variant rows remain; 14,000 normalized rows pass the implemented term checks. This is not an economic/PIT acceptance.

Output: `sources/dividends-ede44a1dd1f049ca82d930e2385155de/audit-f0a0c97f307f46d7a1ff6edbae703a34/normalize-d807b9491579482192f23d4eb06469f8/`.

Primary issuer PDFs explain three of the five remaining reference discrepancies:

- [000697 / 2025-11-28](https://static.cninfo.com.cn/finalpage/2025-11-28/1224831707.PDF): previous close 8.52, special restructuring reference 8.05.
- [002713 / 2025-12-30](https://static.cninfo.com.cn/finalpage/2025-12-30/1224906746.PDF): previous close 11.00, special restructuring reference 8.14.
- [300091 / 2026-02-12](https://static.cninfo.com.cn/finalpage/2026-02-12/1224977289.PDF): previous close 3.16, special restructuring reference 2.79.

All three announcements describe converted shares allocated through administrator accounts to investors/creditors, not a pro-rata bonus to ordinary holders. Thus a vendor `stk_div` rate must NOT automatically increase an ordinary holder's shares. Reference-price explanation does not neutralize actual economic dilution/loss. Added an explicit verified-beneficiary requirement to the ordinary distribution accountant and a restructuring/unknown-beneficiary rejection test.

Reviewed-case facts and sources are in `config/s20_v4_corporate_action_cases.json`. These are retrospective explanatory records, not prediction-time exclusion rules. Actual authoritative historical availability/halt-state evidence is still a dataset integration requirement, not presumed satisfied by this adapter.

## Interrupted terminal-commit recovery — 2026-09-14

Implemented `recovery.py` and CLI `resume`. Recovery holds a registry transaction while checking PID plus process creation time: matching live owners are left alone, access-denied/unknown owners are not presumed dead. A confirmed dead owner can finish a previously written terminal manifest only if all artifacts verify and its hash matches a durable VERIFYING event with matching run/stage identity. Otherwise the interrupted run becomes FAILED_INFRA, retaining files and appending the reason. Repeated reconciliation is idempotent; no collector or model is automatically restarted.

Runtime now persists the VERIFYING manifest-hash event before its terminal registry commit, making that crash boundary recoverable. This is terminal-commit reconciliation, not full per-partition retry/cache recovery: upstream-run binding, bounded retry lineage and stage-level resumable task execution remain required. Recovery does not authorize formal training or convert a failed validity audit to success.

Actual `python -m research.s20_harness.cli resume` exited 0 with `actions=[]`, `training_started=false`: there were no active registry runs to reconcile. Tests cover live/unknown/dead owners, missing manifest failure, successful anchored recovery, tampered manifest rejection and idempotence. No production files or frozen protocol changed; H01/H02 integration and later stages remain incomplete.

Full harness regression after these changes: **117 passed in 19.27s**. All test sessions are terminal.

For record 10-30 and ex/payment 10-31, it explicitly distinguishes **4.2073 per underlying share**, **0.42073 per domestic CDR**, and **0.41873 per CDR for the repurchase-adjusted ex-reference**. The latter is not actual holder cash. Registered CDR holders excluding repurchase account receive the declared CDR entitlement; no bonus/capitalization. This resolves the semantic explanation for that specific event, not all four vendor rows or historical availability.

Also read [official tax notice 2023 No.22](https://fgk.chinatax.gov.cn/zcfgk/c102416/c5211074/content.html): its specified 2023-09-21 through 2025-12-31 personal CDR dividend provisions reference differential holding-period tax, with depositary withholding and potential foreign-tax credits. ## Denominator-preserving evaluation metrics implemented — 2026-09-14

Added `metrics.py` for later H03–H07 consumers: explicit recommendation identities, many-to-one outcome joins and complete evaluation calendar preserve missing outcomes and inactive days. It separately reports all-recommendation and filled-only metrics, unique stocks/samples, episode counts/missing episodes, fill states and coverage. Unknown outcomes yield lower/upper identification bounds, not confidence intervals; empty selections have null rates, never 100% success. No episode independence or portfolio results are invented, and numeric scores cannot masquerade as boolean realized events.

Actual arithmetic check on hash-pinned archived **2026-05-29 full candidate universe**, not a recommendation strategy: 5,190 total/3,597 known/1,593 unknown. Gross-profit known-only rate 22.66%, full-denominator bounds 15.70–46.40%; gross-safe-profit known-only 6.39%, bounds 4.43–35.13%; B5 known-only 92.80%, bounds 64.32–95.01%. These broad ranges quantify missing-label uncertainty under that diagnostic's assumptions; they are not predictive performance or a revised R20/S20 comparison. Only the archived label file hash was needed for this arithmetic audit; current-source compatibility of old partitions remains intentionally invalidated by subsequent review changes.

Tests cover missing joined outcomes, failed fills retaining denominator, inactive dates, nullable empty metrics, duplicate recommendations and nonboolean outcomes. Full regression **138 passed in 22.26s**, session **6647** terminal. Next connect these metrics to matched baseline consumers, add paired date-block inference and complete upstream data/label acceptance. Full H00–H10 remains incomplete; no production mutation or formal model trained.

## Paired full-calendar block diagnostics implemented — 2026-09-14

Added `paired_blocks.py`, consuming the daily event counts of `recommendation_report`. Candidate and baseline must share the exact complete chronological calendar. Noncircular moving blocks resample the same dates on both sides, pool recommendation denominators rather than averaging daily rates, and preserve inactive days. Default block lengths are 20/40/60 with deterministic seed-specific percentile diagnostic intervals. Daily recommendation-count matching is reported, not inferred from equal aggregate counts; this component does not verify a shared candidate universe or episode independence.

Unknown outcomes yield difference identification bounds only. Empty selections, zero/all-event margins, fewer than two complete blocks, any empty resampled denominator, or degenerate resampling refuse a confidence interval. The two-block check is a computational minimum, explicitly not statistical power. Percentile diagnostics alone never authorize formal promotion or guarantee coverage under arbitrary market dependence; power, episode checks and the frozen evaluation protocol remain required.

Four tests cover paired identical series, unknown/inactive days, deterministic nondegenerate intervals, invalid calendars/counts and rare-event protection. Full harness regression **142 passed in 22.44s**, session **21852** terminal. These are synthetic statistical-contract checks, not a new S20 performance result. Next connect real baseline consumers after upstream H01/H02 acceptance; H03+ executors and the full H00–H10 objective remain incomplete. No production changes or formal model fit in this turn.

## Transactional pipeline budget reservation — 2026-09-14

Added trial_budget.py: immutable SQLite budget contract, finite pinned trial inputs/attempt counts and separate model/underlying/calibrator/policy counters. BEGIN IMMEDIATE serializes reservations so concurrent jobs cannot overspend. Duplicate attempt IDs do not launch again; failed/reserved attempts remain charged, with no refund. ## Full regression and read-only evaluation CLI — 2026-09-14

Ran the whole harness test directory before further code edits: **588 passed in 166.36s**, session1932 terminal exit0. This supersedes the previous full-suite count, but is evidence of covered engineering contracts, not real stock predictive performance or H01 semantic validity.

Then added the read-only CLI entry:

```powershell
python -m research.s20_harness.cli verify-campaign-evaluation --directory <evaluation-directory> --summary-sha256 <externally-pinned-summary-sha256>
```

The command reconstructs the campaign evaluation via its verifier, emits JSON and returns 0 on valid evidence or 2 on invalid evidence. It does not instantiate stage execution, refit models, or promote formal stage state.
